- **pm-next**: Return the next runnable planning task as JSON, or "null" if the queue is empty / blocked. Parents must be claimed before their children; the rollup-after-children invariant is enforced at finish-time, not next-time. Use when the user says "next task", "what's next", "pull next", or before spawning a worker. ([in8finity/hashharness-pm](https://github.com/in8finity/hashharness-pm/tree/HEAD/skills/pm/next))
